Root cause = the equalize sidecar sets the sidebar height ASYNC via layout (not an inline-style write, hence `writes=0` while the rect still moves), so a before/after-baseline compare races a mount-time transient (`~1000` un-equalized → `~985` settled) that swings ~15px between runs on identical bytes — same class as DIAG-CPH-STORM-DRIFT-FLAKE-001 (08-01). Fix = assert height CONVERGENCE (poll ≤12×150ms until two consecutive reads agree; a real storm never reaches a fixed point so detection is NOT weakened; the `writes<=2` bound untouched). A CI sweep already ran earlier today (`7ed22c1`) on byte-identical code — `git diff --stat 988bb0e6..HEAD` over `pwa/app.js`/`pwa/app.src.js`/`workers/**`/`.github/` is **empty**; this desktop run adds desktop-side confirmation + live attest + a diag reliability fix. **Standing probes GREEN on desktop:** PWA suite **1570/1570** (~15s), `app.js` head `(()=>{window` (0 `"use strict"`), docx render V&V **50/50**. **Render-gated diags (Playwright, desktop) GREEN:** settings-panels probe (row 17) **DIAG PASS** (Layout 0 mut/6s, rootFound=true), sidebar-stable (row 11) **OK** (width/height stable/≤2 writes/12 scrolls/0 err), sidebar-promote-margin (row 11) **OK** (hold-under-margin true across one-row + whole-group removal), panel button-audit (row 23) **215 buttons / 0 page errors / 0 THROWS / 136 active** (14 skipped-dangerous, 13 ui-only, 52 not-visible/disabled; record `PANEL_BUTTON_AUDIT_2026-08-01.*` desktop re-run, count 208→215 vs CI = expected React-remount enumeration variance on identical bytes). **ONE FIX SHIPPED — DIAG-CPH-STORM-DRIFT-FLAKE-001 (test-infra only, no product code, no version, no deploy):** `diag-copenhagen-overflow-storm.mjs` (the standing nightly overflow-storm probe) **false-FAILed on the first desktop run** — ON writes=3/drift=945px, OFF writes=2/drift=915px — yet a second run on the SAME commit read ON 15px/OFF 0px (PASS). Root cause: `drift = u[last] - u[0]` captured a mount-time pre-layout transient in `u[0]` that swings hundreds of px between runs on identical bytes (desktop first-write lands pre-layout, CI/warm run lands settled). Decisive proof it was NOT a band-driven storm: (a) the **write count** — the diag's own documented decisive anti-storm signal (a real climb = 35 writes, since 364 emits one write per distinct usablePx) — stayed bounded at **1-3** on every run; (b) OFF (band KILLED) showed the SAME ~915px drift as ON, so killing the band did not remove the drift. Code is byte-identical to what CI verified green hours ago, so this is definitionally an environment/timing flake, not a regression. Fix: compute drift over the SETTLED tail (`u.slice(1)`), excluding the single leading mount transient; write-count bound left untouched (a real 35-write/435px+ runaway still fails it AND shows a large tail drift, so detection is not weakened). Verified stable **PASS ×3** post-fix (ON 2-3 writes/0-30px, OFF 1-2/0 — the 30px is the documented healthy one-time mount-settle). `node --check` clean; diag is standalone (not in `run-tests.mjs` → suite unaffected). **LIVE ATTEST — all 5 surfaces readable + match repo source:** PWA `sw.js` CACHE `antcv-1.51.4046-company-retry` (= seed + TARGET), access-relay `auth-37-cap-disposable-only`, cv-proxy + demo-proxy `3.8.4-brand-ink-match`, docx-worker `1.14.174-appline-edit` (1042 hostname trap stays resolved — prefixed `antcv-` hosts for access-relay/demo-proxy). **Staleness (E1):** rows **1 + 3** (were 2026-07-31) re-verified against current code → advanced to 2026-08-01 (row 1: empty code delta + all render diags green + live attest; row 3: float-spine gate default-OFF re-confirmed, docx-worker:24668 + docx-client:1253 line refs stable); rows 11/17/23 diags re-run green desktop tonight (held/refreshed); rows 35/36/37 already reconciled to 2026-08-01 by today's CI sweep. **NO product code merged.** Report: `docs/qa/NIGHTLY_2026-08-01_DESKTOP_REPORT.md`.

Editable spans `stopPropagation` on click/mousedown so editing never triggers the row's load-on-switch. Relay unchanged (partial-PUT whitelist already accepts these fields). Edited `pwa/app.src.js` + mirrored to minified `pwa/app.js` (var map: `zl→Ql io→So lo→Ro u→L oo→vo Gl→ls Fl→es`); `node --check` clean on both; cache-bust quintet applied (index.html `app.js?v` + `ANTCV_VERSION` seed, `sw.js` CACHE, `antcv-version-override.js` TARGET; previous `1.51.3982-inline-edit-appline` added to STALE_VERSIONS, new version NOT). > **HDR-TYPE-CONTROLS-001 — DONE / SHIPPED (2026-07-29 desktop session, Opus 5; PWA `1.51.3862-hdr-type-ctrl`, docx-worker `1.14.173-hdr-type-ctrl` DEPLOYED (run 30450517787, `/health`-confirmed)).** Owner: the panel must control SIZE **and** compression/expansion (letter spacing, 0.05 steps, signed) for **Name, Specialisation, Application, Contact line and Slogan**, in all modes, on the CV **and** the CL, in preview **and** export — "and make sure nothing prevents the user from controlling these values"; Specialisation and Application must be SEPARATE controls (they are no longer in the same place) and the Slogan needs one at all. **Shipped.** New `fontSizes` keys: `applicationSize`, `sloganSize` (sizes) + `nameTrack`, `specTrack`, `applicationTrack`, `contactTrack`, `sloganTrack` (letter-spacing DELTAS in pt). 0.05pt = exactly one twentieth of a point = the DOCX `w:spacing` unit, so a panel step maps onto Word character spacing losslessly; DELTA semantics mean 0 = unchanged, so every existing document renders byte-identically. Panel: `Specialisation / Application` split into two rows, `Slogan` row added, a signed ±0.05 letter-spacing control on each of the five identity rows, plus an `All Identity Lines ↔` bulk row. **The "nothing prevents the user" sweep found four real blockers, all fixed:** (a) `antcv-docx-client.js` `buildFontSizes()` is a WHITELIST and dropped all seven new keys — every control would have been silently inert on the DOCX/PDF leg; (b) the copenhagen measured fit emits `letter-spacing … !important` for the name with no panel escape — the panel deltas are now emitted AFTER the fit rules so the owner is the last word; (c) the docx-worker hard-pinned the CL slogan at 11pt/`characterSpacing` 20 and the application line at 10.5pt/4 — both read the payload now, and `__cphNameFit` folds the name delta into its fitted track; (d) `antcv-pdf-preview-gate.js`'s fallback export path read `personalInfo.fontSizes` (a legacy mirror that is usually absent) instead of the canonical `localStorage.fontSizes`, so that path shipped with NO owner sizing. **Bug found on the way — APPLINE-PARITY-001:** the CL application line was **11px in the preview and 10.5pt (14px) in the export**, a 27% preview/export split; both legs are 10.5pt now and read the same key (the slogan's 15px-vs-11pt split is likewise closed at 11pt). Wired across 30 sites in `pwa/app.js` (prelude helpers on `window`, defaults ×3, tracking stepper, panel, live preview band, live preview CL slogan + app line, the `name_block`/`spec_block`/`contact_line` figure-layout leg preview + HTML, HTML export band, HTML export CL, in-app DOCX stylesheet `<w:spacing>`), the copenhagen sidecar, the docx client, the pdf-preview gate and the docx-worker. **Verified:** PWA suite **1504/1504**, workers **290/290**, render V&V **50/50** docx diags incl. the new `diag-hdr-type-controls.mjs` (12 checks — every key reaches its run, each track shifts `w:spacing` by exactly pt×20, and an all-zero payload is byte-identical to no payload); `node --check` clean; cache-bust quintet applied and asserted by test (STALE_VERSIONS invariant respected). **MIRRORED into `pwa/app.src.js`** (31 sites; corrects an earlier call in this session not to). The file is NOT stale — a marker census shows every one of the 68 ticket markers in `app.js` is present there, i.e. sessions have kept it covered; only THIS change was missing. Its short names come from an older minifier pass (fontSizes is `Yr` there, `ca` in `app.js`), so mirroring is by MEANING, by hand — not mechanical. **Regenerating it from `app.js` was tried and rejected:** a prettier pass gated on AST equivalence produced a provably identical program, but the mirror is the SOLE home of ~376 ticket-marked design rationales that minification strips (444 markers in `app.src.js` vs 68 in `app.js`), and it broke 74 assertions across 43 test files that anchor on those comments. New mirror-lock tests now pin key-count parity and assert the rationale set may never shrink. **Deployed and live-verified:** PWA `app.js?v=1.51.3862-hdr-type-ctrl` + `sw.js` CACHE served from antcv.pages.dev with `__antcvTrkStep` present in the deployed bundle; docx-worker `/health` = `1.14.173-hdr-type-ctrl` (dispatch run 30450517787, PWA push run first, one-deployer rule respected). Full-stack diagnosis: **already FIXED by `OPEN-JD-VISIBLE-001` (2026-07-12)** and live across every layer — `jd_text` (the real JD) and `supporting_context` (Dream-Envelope + target-facts + research + owner signals) are SEPARATE fields; on restore (`app.src.js` cold-start path ~L17703 + Read-from-Cloud path ~L23392) ONLY the `ADDITIONAL SIGNALS (owner-added)` block is lifted into the signals textarea (`Vt`), and `jd_text` seeds the drop-zone (`Ft`/`Dt`, renders ~L43429). Audited all 18 `Vt(...)` calls — **none receives `jd_text`**. Confirmed in `app.src.js`, deployed `app.js` (canonical regex present at both restore sites), `JobTracker.tsx` (`prepareAndOpen` sends `jd_text: jd`, JD never folded into `supporting_context`), and built `antcv-react-islands.js`. Suite **1482 → 1491**, all green. **Zero runtime change** (module not in the `src/main-core.tsx` build graph → `antcv-react-islands.js` byte-unchanged), no cache-bust, no version bump. > **JOBTRACKER-EMIT-SCHEMA-TRUNCATE-001 — DONE (2026-07-25 job-tracker nightly, worktree-isolated).** `build_workbook.py`'s doc-emit step overwrote the local `job_tracker_doc.json` with a 7-key literal on every `pull --render`, silently dropping `brand`/`brandfit`/`queue`/`signals`/`webintel`/`discovered`/`gen`/`pin`/`park` — a latent data-loss bug (an unlucky `push`/`import-xlsx` right after a render would have PUT the truncated doc to the cloud verbatim, wiping those keys; the cloud doc itself was never actually corrupted, only exposed). Fixed by seeding `_doc_out` from `dict(_DOC)` before applying the script's own overrides, so every other key round-trips. Verified: post-fix `pull --render` retains all 16 doc keys (rev 154). Found while checking an owner report of missing brand colours on recent generations — that report traced to a client-side issue already fixed same-day (BRAND-EVERYWHERE, PWA `1.51.3763`); the schema-truncation bug is a separate, independent finding from the same investigation. Detail: ACTIVE_BUGS top entry 2026-07-25 (job-tracker).

> **DIAG-SALMON-EMPTY-REGION-STALE-001 — OPEN, report-only debt (found 2026-07-26 CI nightly, Opus 4.8; render-domain; NOT a regression).** The standalone diag `pwa/test/diag-salmon-empty-region.mjs` (last touched 2026-06-20, `1.50.753`; NOT in CI / not in `run-tests.mjs` → gates nothing) FAILS on current main (`1.51.3803`). **Root-caused (Playwright probe, diagnostic-first): STALE FIXTURE + OBSOLETE ASSERTIONS, not a live pagination bug.** (1) The 5-role fixture's main content measures **1304px** against the CURRENT usable preview page height **1275px** → `antcv:mainOverflow` verdict = **"squeeze"** (overshoot 29px / 1.8 lines), so the engine correctly keeps it to **ONE** page. The diag was calibrated for the old ~1053px usable page (CLAUDE.md "preview line ~1053px") where 1304px was clearly 2 pages; the usable height has since grown, so the fixture now lands in the small-overshoot single-page squeeze band → `pageRows=1`, failing all 4 asserts. (2) Its assertion "LAST page-row keeps full A4 (1123px)" is obsolete by design twice over: `antcv-page-fit.js` collapses even the last multi-page row to content (`rows.length===1 ? sheetHeightPx() : '0px'`), AND PREVIEW-SHEET-WORD-HEIGHT-001 (`1.51.3803`) made the single/last sheet the Word-equivalent height (~985px = 1123/1.14), not true A4. **No corroborating regression signal:** in-CI salmon/storm guards green (suite 1482/1482), copenhagen-storm + sidebar + settings + button diags all PASS, all workers attest live. **Repair recipe (owed to a render-capable/desktop session — salmon = most blue-screen-prone per CLAUDE.md, and the diag asserts preview↔Word-export parity that needs a real render to confirm):** bump the fixture past the squeeze band (≈7–8 roles so content clearly > 1275px, verdict "break"); drop the obsolete "last row = 1123" assert and instead assert every page-row (incl. last) collapses to its content (no dead A4 gap — the true SALMON-EMPTY-REGION invariant that survived); keep the flush-salmon-gap check. Then optionally wire into `run-tests.mjs`. Same class as DOCX-DIAG-STALE-OR-REGRESSED-001. PRE-EXISTING on main. **UPDATE 2026-07-26 (desktop nightly, Opus 4.8) — attempted the CI recipe; it is INSUFFICIENT, reverted the WIP, diagnosis deepened (still OPEN):** bumping to 7–8 roles does NOT produce a 2-page render in this headless harness — the preview pagination MEASUREMENT pass (which populates `antcv:autoPagesPreview` / sets the render's page count `u`) does not run to completion in the seeded `step:'editor'` context within the wait window, so even ~2× a page of content (measured 2015px) stays `pageRows=1` (`u=Math.max(1,...pages)` defaults to 1). Two further fixture bugs surfaced: (a) the fixture `profile` renders as an empty "(click to add)" placeholder — the current build no longer reads its `text` field, so page 1 is near-empty; (b) forcing the split via manual `role.page` (which DOES win over the measurer in the render role→page map `d`) exposes the measurer partially running and reordering roles across 3 page-boxes. A clean, stable, representative 2-page render is NOT reliably reproducible headlessly — proper repair needs the in-app Browser pane driving the real Preview tab + awaiting the measurer, or a fully deterministic seeded `autoPagesPreview` map + the profile-field fix. Filed not shipped (a fragile half-working test would violate "an end result, not a brickable mid product"). Gates nothing (not in CI / run-tests.mjs). | 2026-07-26 | OPEN — deeper: headless harness can't paginate; needs in-app Preview |

Grower = the `antcv-sidebar-fill-equalize-227`/`antcv-page-fit` chain: with the band present `mainH` drifts up each cycle so the equalize idempotency guard never holds; 364's osc-damp only catches a two-state flip, not a monotone climb. **Bisect:** converged (1 write) at `1.51.1972`/`c77e38f2`, runaway (35 writes) at `c336c5a5` (Stage-3 header band, `1.51.3061`) — the introducing commit; `antcv:copenhagen-v2=0` restores convergence on HEAD. Default-ON since 3061 → affects every user on the default template. Detected by `diag-settings-panels-probe.mjs` (row 17) DIAG-FAIL on the `antcv:mainOverflow` set-count. Permanent repro `pwa/test/diag-copenhagen-overflow-storm.mjs`. NOT fixed in CI (preview-layout / salmon area = blue-screen-prone, not live-verifiable from CI) — fix owed to a render-capable/desktop session. | 39 | RELAY-TUNE-NAN-FALLBACK-001 (found + FIXED by weekly cost-quality tune 2026-07-22): `scoreRows` in `scripts/relay-cost-quality-tune.mjs` guarded the quality composite on `Number(r.health_score) != null`. Since `Number(undefined) === NaN` and `NaN != null` is `true`, the intended fallback to `success_rate` NEVER fired when a snapshot lacked `health_score` — poisoning `quality` (and `costQuality`) with NaN. This is exactly the offline snapshot the routine builds from D1 `llm_calls` (no `health_score` column) whenever the relay admin token is unavailable (the 2026-07-22 and 2026-07-15 runs). Fix: guard on the raw field (`r.health_score != null ? Number(...) : Number(r.success_rate) || 0`) + a covering test ("missing health_score falls back to success_rate"). Pure scoring-script change, no deploy, no version consumed; suite 14→15 green. | `scripts/relay-cost-quality-tune.mjs:92` + `scripts/tests/relay-cost-quality-tune.test.mjs`; COST_QUALITY_WEEKLY_2026-07-22.md | DONE 2026-07-22 (docs/script only) |

Fix = `openModal()` now shows a lightweight loading shell IMMEDIATELY (the click handler itself is cheap DOM creation only — no more long-task attributed to the click) and builds the real modal on the next frame (double `requestAnimationFrame`, so the loading shell has actually painted first); `buildModal()`'s own "remove any existing backdrop" step at its top tears the loading shell down for free (same id), verified via a headless DOM-mechanics test (immediate spinner, exactly one backdrop after settle, no duplicate/leak). This does NOT shrink the total synchronous cost — it turns "frozen, no feedback" into "spinner appears immediately, then builds" — reducing the underlying cost (e.g. `cloneNode` + direct iframe DOM insertion instead of an `outerHTML`/`srcdoc` round-trip) is the likely next lever but needs live DevTools profiling against a real multi-page document before touching this build path further. **REMAINING — the `'setTimeout' handler took ~3270ms` leg (antcv-generate-cloud-sync-277.js) is still OPEN**: traced the code (not just guessed) — the setTimeout callback itself only constructs and dispatches a synthetic click on the real Generate button; `dispatchEvent` is synchronous, so the ~3.27s is almost certainly attributable to whatever app.js's OWN generate-click handler does synchronously before yielding, not to this sidecar. Fixing that requires either live profiling to confirm, or editing app.js — both out of scope for a diagnostic-first sidecar-only pass; the sidecar's own JSON parse/stringify calls (sections/meta/personalInfo, which can carry a photo data URI) were checked and are plausible but not confirmed as the dominant cost | owner backlog 2026-07-04 | partial — 1.51.158 |

(2) The click-listener attach was gated on a bare module-level boolean (`topbarUndoWired`), not a per-node marker — if the app ever replaces the button element (e.g. on its disabled→enabled transition) rather than updating it in place, the flag stayed `true` from a previous sight and the fresh node got no listener at all; clicks silently did nothing. | **50** | **UPLOAD-SCREEN-TOP-CLIP-001 (owner 2026-07-05, same live session as rows 46-49):** the upload screen's EN/gear/Editor header row was still cut off at the top despite the earlier UPLOAD-SCREEN-SCROLLTOP-001 attempt (row 46's fix, `ref: el => el.scrollTop = 0`), and a SECOND symptom appeared live: while a background "Generating kernel showcase…" run was active, the same header row rendered BEHIND the fixed progress banner instead of below it. Live CDP measurement on the real phone proved the scrollTop-forcing fix was chasing the wrong mechanism — the header row sat at a NEGATIVE offset (top:-13px) even with scrollTop already 0, so it was never a scroll-position bug. Root cause: the upload screen's outer `.fade` container (`"upload"===Nt`, app.src.js) sets `justifyContent:"center"` on a flex column whose content is TALLER than one viewport — centering overflowing content clips the excess symmetrically off BOTH the top and bottom. The inner content wrapper's `margin:"auto 0"` (added for row 46) already resolves to 0 once content overflows per the flexbox spec (correct top-anchor-and-scroll), but the outer `justifyContent:"center"` was still fighting it. Confirmed live before touching source: toggling `justifyContent` on the real DOM node from "center" to "flex-start" moved the header row from top:-13px (off-screen, no banner) / top:38px (behind the banner's own 0-58px span, banner active) to a clean top:79-79px, clear of both cases. **SHIPPED 1.51.189** — surgical edit in both app.src.js and the minified app.js mirror (unique-string-verified single occurrence in each via Node script + vm.Script parse-gate, since app.js's single-line minified body exceeds the Edit tool's read limit): `justifyContent:"center"` → `justifyContent:"flex-start"` on the upload-screen `.fade` block; also removed the now-confirmed-ineffective `ref: el => el.scrollTop = 0` (dead code from the superseded row-46 fix — scrollTop was already 0, so it never did anything). New `upload-screen-top-clip.test.mjs` (4 tests) locks the fix + the dead-code removal in both files. Full suite 1160/1160 green. | **51** | **PREVIEW-SCROLL-JITTER-001 (owner 2026-07-05, live session, reported as two symptoms: "application analysis panel is stuck again, does not scroll" + "the preview is a bit shaky, very strong jitter — you can see it between two pages"):** live-verified BOTH symptoms share one root cause. The preview's fit-recompute effect (`_i()` in app.src.js, `Gi()` in the minified mirror) unconditionally snaps the shared preview/analysis scroll container back to `(0,0)` (immediate + 80ms + 240ms staggered `scrollTo`), and used to re-fire on ANY change to `[Lt, je, Ke, ya]` (doc type, language, navyColor, styleConfig) with no check on which view was active or whether the user was mid-scroll. Lt/je (doc/language) are deliberate user choices — resetting to top on those is correct and intentional. Ke/ya (navyColor/styleConfig) are PURELY COSMETIC and change silently in the background (brand-fit, the STYLE-BG-FOLLOW-PKG-001 self-heal effect, cloud-restore) — a colour/font change has no reason to move the user's reading position. Live-proven via CDP on the real device before touching source: scrolled the preview to `scrollTop:500` (into page 2), then triggered a real style-package change through the app's own reset API (`window.AntcvPackageState.write`, the same code path a Settings click uses) — `scrollTop` dropped to 209 within ~1s with zero user interaction, reproducing the "shaky, between two pages" snap. The Analysis panel's "stuck, does not scroll" symptom shares the exact same container and effect (a separate, correctly-gated effect at app.src.js ~18864 already resets scroll on `("preview"===ei||"analysis"===ei)` view-switch, which is fine; the broken effect had no such gate and fired regardless of which view was showing). **SHIPPED 1.51.192** — removed `Ke, ya` from the effect's dependency array in both app.src.js and the minified app.js mirror (unique-string-verified, vm.Script parse-gate); `_i()`'s own body is untouched, so doc/language-triggered resets still work. | **61** | **LINE-DISTRIBUTION-GUIDELINES-001 (KOMBIT lessons v1→v7, owner asked to crystallize)** — conclusions on line-fill / orphan control, the single most-iterated pain this session: **(1) Line fill is a RENDER property, not a character count** — it depends on font, size, column width (twips), kerning, hyphenation and nbsp; char count is a weak proxy in a proportional font ("iii"≠"WWW"). You CANNOT fit lines without rendering + measuring; every blind pass (v1-v7) missed. **(2) It is BIDIRECTIONAL** — two failure modes: RUNT (a wrapped block whose LAST line is 1-2 short words / <~60% fill) and SHORT (a line ending well before the margin, looks empty). Fix a runt by TRIMMING (wrap one less time, or grow the runt) OR ENRICHING; fix a short line by ENRICHING. Never trim-only. **(3) The unit is the LAST LINE's fill ratio, not total length** — a 3-line bullet is fine if line 3 is full; target the last line into a good band (~0.6-0.95 of the column content width). **(4) Layout BEFORE fill — and Fit-it is the RE-FIT tool.** A column-width change (sidebar 2.31"→2.4"), a hide, or a delete reflows every line and staleness the fill. The generator fits to the layout at gen time (best-effort); the user then changes layout/content, and that is EXACTLY what the **Fit-it / fit-width** function is for — an on-demand re-fit applied AFTER the change (owner 2026-07-07). So (a) the generator fits as well as possible up front, AND (b) Fit-it MUST re-measure against the CURRENT column widths + currently-visible content and re-distribute BIDIRECTIONALLY (trim runts + enrich shorts) — it must stay correct after any width/hide/delete, never assuming the original layout. Ties memories fixit-orphan-enhance-orchestration (multi-LLM sample-and-pick via ee()) + orphan-measure-bind: Fit-it = re-run the orphan-measure + enrich/trim pass against live state, decoupled from generation. **(5) Result lines = one-line budget** — must fit EXACTLY one full line (no wrap) AND state the mechanism; trim to the column's one-line max. **(6) Levers beyond rewording** — nbsp to keep/pull a trailing word, justified alignment (only stretches a line that ALREADY wraps — a single fitting line won't justify), hyphenation, or a small width nudge; rewording (trim/enrich) is primary. **(7) It is the GENERATOR's job** — a pre-paginated export can't reflow and hand-editing can't measure; the orphan-measure pass (renders line boxes, adjusts) is the right home (rows 27/49/59, memory orphan-measure-bind, RUNT_INVENTORY). **(8) Owner char-deltas ("+18/−4") are a symptom of eyeballing the render** — the METHOD is to measure the render, not chase char counts. **(9) Gold standard** (memory trackman-gold-target): no 2-3-word runts, last lines reach the margin, columns bottom out together, no blank under the sidebar. **(10) "AT BEST" HAS A FLOOR — never ship a VISIBLE LEAK** (owner 2026-07-07). Best-effort fill is NOT licence for a visibly-wrong result. Floor: (a) CUTS must be CLEAN — end at a word/clause/sentence boundary, NEVER truncate a word or strand a dangling connector ("og"/"med"/"via"/"&"/"-") or half-phrase; a visible truncation is worse than a slightly short/long line. (b) MISSING FILLERS are ALSO visible leaks — a line ending glaringly short (empty-looking) or a 1-2-word runt is a DEFECT, not merely "not ideal". So the generator prioritises ZERO visible defects FIRST, then optimises fill within that: a clean-but-imperfect line beats a truncated or obviously-empty one. Cuts are the more obvious offender, but short fillers count too. **WORD RENDER AVAILABLE — the definitive check (2026-07-07, fully resolves row 59C):** `WINWORD.EXE` is installed; render docx→PDF with the ACTUAL Word engine via PowerShell COM (`$w=New-Object -ComObject Word.Application; $d=$w.Documents.Open($docx,$false,$true); $d.ExportAsFixedFormat($pdf,17)`), then Read the PDF to VERIFY real pagination, colour, table fit, and orphans. `$d.ComputeStatistics(2)` returns page count. This beats font-metric estimation (which is still useful for fast pre-tuning) — always render + eyeball before shipping. **MULTI-LANGUAGE:** the Word render is language-agnostic + definitive (does shaping/BiDi/CJK) — use it for EN/ES/DA/中文/עברית/العربية. The font-metric estimator is FONT+LANGUAGE specific: Latin = word-wrap on the doc's font; CJK = character-wrap + a CJK font (no inter-word spaces); Hebrew/Arabic = use the render (Arabic contextual shaping + BiDi make isolated-glyph `getlength` unreliable). **CloudConvert (LibreOffice) is STILL required for the PRODUCTION export** (no Word on Cloudflare/in-browser); local Word render is dev/verification only + matches the user's Word view. **BROWSER-PREVIEW corollary (ties row 60 Fit-it + orphan-measure-bind):** the app's preview + Fit-it/orphan pass should MEASURE the live DOM (`getClientRects`/canvas `measureText`, device font) PER-DEVICE (desktop vs mobile wrap differently), not estimate — same "measure don't guess" principle improves preview↔export fidelity on desktop + mobile Chrome. **BULLET-GLYPH COLOUR lives in `numbering.xml` (`<w:lvl><w:rPr><w:color>`), NOT `document.xml`** — a doc-only recolor left the bullets the old teal `00746E` (looks greenish); brand recolor MUST include numbering.xml. **THEME-COLOUR TRAP:** setting `w:color w:val` does NOT override a `w:themeColor`/`w:themeTint` on the same element (Word resolves the theme colour; the AI-notice rendered grey despite val=B0241A until themeColor/themeTint were stripped) — colour-verify in the RENDER (PyMuPDF span colour), not the XML. **Delivered PDFs use the job-specific filename matching the docx, not scratch _render_ names.** **MEASUREMENT — FONT-METRIC METHOD WORKS HERE (2026-07-07, unblocks row 59C):** no LibreOffice needed — Pillow + `C:\Windows\Fonts\calibri.ttf` measures text width; body font = **Calibri 10pt** (docDefault Calibri 10.5, body runs sz=20=10pt). Calibrated: the KERNEKOMPETENCER Strategisk column content ("AI use cases til drift: krav, evaluering, monitorering, sikkerhed") = 3.50in = exactly 1 line at the 3.65in column → confirms Calibri 10pt. Main-column bullet text width — **CALIBRATE AGAINST REALITY, don't trust the geometry estimate**: (8450−300−210−360)/2 = 3790 (5.26in) was ~0.3in TOO WIDE (nested-cell margins + bullet indent eat more), so the model under-counted lines and two bullets spilled → a page skip. Real width = **3560 (pt×10) ≈ 4.94in**, pinned by observed wrap (CCB=3 lines, PROFIL=5 lines at the too-wide estimate). Always calibrate the width against an actual render or the user's observed line-count before tuning. Greedy-wrap each line, compute last-line fillRatio, tune text to ~0.85−0.96 (no runt). Ends the blind char-delta guessing (v1−v9). **FONT-SPECIFIC (owner 2026-07-07):** always read the doc's ACTUAL font (docDefault rFonts + run sz, or the selected package/style) and load the matching `.ttf` before measuring — a style/package swap (Trebuchet MS=`trebuc.ttf`, Segoe UI=`segoeui.ttf`, Arial=`arial.ttf`, …) breaks the Calibri metrics; re-load that font + re-calibrate against a known-fit line. Never hardcode Calibri. **KERNEKOMP hard limits (owner):** Fokusområde ≤1.59in (2290tw), Strategisk ekspertise ≤3.65in (5256tw) — larger overflows the visible region, smaller wraps the cell text; set exactly + fixed layout. **ALT (PDF) RECIPE:** `soffice --headless --convert-to pdf`; per column, extract each text line's bbox width → fillRatio = lineW/colContentW; flag last-line fillRatio<0.6 (enrich), any block that spills an extra line vs intent (trim), any result that wraps (trim to 1 line); iterate until every block's last line lands ~0.6-0.95 and results are 1 line. | KOMBIT v1-v7 (2026-07-07) | Guidelines — feed the generator orphan-measure pass (row 59A) |

Root cause: the mobile bottom panel's sticky drag handle (`.antcv-panel-grab-zone`, 28px, top of the panel, directly above the scrollable Sections/Edit/Analysis tab content) used its own 28px height as its swipe-down-to-dismiss threshold, so a touch starting on/near the strip read any small downward slide as "dismiss to preview" (hiding the WHOLE candidate editor — the "contact section collapses the entire candidate" symptom) instead of reaching the content below. **SHIPPED (threshold 28→80px)**, then a **same-day follow-up (author outside this session, found on re-verify 2026-07-29) fixed a second half of the same bug**: raising the threshold alone did not restore scrolling, because `touchAction:"none"` on the handle blocks the browser's OWN native scroll for ANY touch starting there, independent of the JS threshold — the first fix only delayed the dismiss, it never let a non-dismiss drag actually scroll. GRAB-ZONE-SCROLL-FORWARD-001 manually forwards the incremental per-move delta to the active tab's scrollable content (`nextElementSibling.scrollTop`) whenever the gesture hasn't crossed the dismiss threshold. **Both legs confirmed present and stacked correctly in `pwa/app.src.js`/`pwa/app.js` on current `main` 2026-07-29** — the bug as originally reported should be fully resolved, not just partially. ## CLOSED (evidence; most recent first)

- **2026-07-22 — LANG-GUARD-KEY-001: generation no longer "starts in Chinese" (PWA `1.51.2200`, eb36a8e, live-diagnosed).** The three loss-guards (CL prose / outcomes / core competencies) cache the last real generated content keyed by `company|role` and re-inject it during a later generation’s skeleton window. They were LANGUAGE-BLIND, so a Chinese cache poisoned a fresh English generation ("starts in Chinese, switches to English") — the generation-time leg of the same family as APP-SWITCH-CONTENT-LANG-DETECT-001 (`1.51.2012`, selector-side). Fix = append the current output language to each guard’s cache key (`company|role|lang`) so a generation only restores from a same-language snapshot; the stale lang-less zh caches no longer match. Test `lang-guard-key.test.mjs` (12) + updated guard behavioural tests; suite 1401/1401; live-verified the new `…|en` key does not match the CJK caches.

- **2026-07-22 — EXPORT-PREVIEW-ZOOM-DECOUPLE-001: export preview no longer follows the editor zoom and stops chopping pages ~half (PWA `1.51.2085`, 745b88d, live-verified).** One root cause: the editor bakes its zoom as an inline `transform: scale()` on `.antcv-preview-paper` (`app.src.js` ~51102) and the export modal clones the paper via `p.outerHTML`, so the clone inherited the zoom AND double-scaled it against the modal’s own fit zoom (a transform’s layout box stays full-size → page-row desync → half-page clip). Fix = `.antcv-preview-paper { transform: none !important }` in the gate srcdoc `<style>` (beats the inline transform); the preview now fits independently via body `zoom: var(--antcv-fit)`. Test `export-preview-zoom-decouple.test.mjs`; suite 1378/1378; live on deployed 2085: computed transform `none` (DECOUPLED) with editor at `scale(0.955)`, single fit `zoom=0.512`.

- **2026-07-22 — HTML-TO-PDF-001: JD-analysis report exports via CloudConvert (PWA `1.51.2072`, docx-worker `1.14.162`, shipped + live-verified).** Owner: "add the analysis to export with CloudConvert." The analysis report printed via offscreen-iframe `window.print()` (ATS-illegible, no ToUnicode CMap); now it renders server-side through the docx-worker’s new `POST /generate-analysis-pdf` (CloudConvert Chrome HTML→PDF, `convertHtmlToPdf`), same gates + BYOK as `/generate-pdf`. PWA `exportPdf()` tries the worker first (gated by `__antcvUseServerPdf`+`isPdfWorkerAvailable`) and keeps the browser-print path as `printViaIframe()` fallback. Test `html-to-pdf.test.mjs`; worker CI 17/17; PWA 1365/1365; live `/health` `1.14.162-analysis-html-pdf`; direct POST = 200 `application/pdf` 30KB; driven e2e on the deployed PWA — POSTed to `/generate-analysis-pdf`, downloaded the PDF, `window.print` 0 calls.

  - **JOBTRACKER-AUTOFILL-TOP5-001 (1.51.388, commit 82d6eaa; spec docs/qa/JOBTRACKER_AUTOFILL_TOP5_SPEC.md)** — new `src/islands/JobTracker/rank.ts` (pure `computeTier`/`fitScore`/`orderTop5`, unit-tested 9/9) wired into JobTracker.tsx. (1) Top-5 is now DETERMINISTIC fit-ranked (pins first, then highest fitScore; closed + parked excluded), recomputed on every doc change so any add re-evaluates against the current Top-5. (2) Pin / Park / Reject split replaces the single "drop": Pin forces into Top-5 (stays in list); Park removes from Top-5 but keeps the row LIVE (not archived); Reject archives WITH a reason → envelope learning AND writes `doc.discovered[key]` so the discovery task never re-proposes it. (3) AUTO-TIER manual adds via `computeTier` (location gate → domain tokens → cluster blend) + learn-from-prior gen-tier + "Review & tailor" next-step. (4) Async LLM refine on add (rides analyzeJd): tier upgrade + extracts location/salary/deadline/hiring-manager + tailored next-step + envelope-conflict flags + auto brand-colour sample; deterministic baseline is the fallback, never fabricates, never blocks. LIVE-VERIFIED on antcv.pages.dev non-destructively (island boots 1.51.388 no errors; 23 rows + 5 cards each with Pin/Park/Reject; Top-5 fit-ranked — rank #6 surfaced above 4/5; Park refills by fit; doc left clean at rev 54). - **[CLOSED 2026-07-29, all layers — gen prompt OPENING-MANDATORY 1.51.4045 + sidecar fallback + load fixes 3962/3983; see SESSION_LOG_2026-07-29 §7]** **CL-OPENING-EMPTY-GEN-001** (owner 2026-07-29, "fix — proxy/gen-flow, owed") — the CL **opening** (rich_block) is persisted EMPTY at GENERATION (distinct from the LOAD bug fixed in UNSOL-CL-LOAD-HYDRATE-001 / CL-OPENING-SEED-985). Root cause: the opening skeleton item is a bracketed instruction (`[OPENING & APPLICATION CONTEXT …]`, app.src.js:4634); when the LLM's opening is rejected as placeholder-like (prose-guard bracket-LED detection) or the model omits the slot, it's cleared to "" and — with no prior real opening — stays blank. **Fix (proxy/writing-engine, workers/proxy CL gen):** guarantee the `opening` section is generated and VALIDATED non-empty (retry the slot if empty) and NEVER persist an empty/placeholder opening; if the model still returns nothing, fall back to a real JD-derived opener ("Following my interest in <role> at <company>, …") rather than "". **Interim client guard (PWA, lower-risk):** in `antcv-cl-prose-loss-guard-985.js`, if the opening is empty/placeholder AND no snapshot exists, build a fallback opener from `meta.role`+`meta.company` so an empty opening never renders/exports. Repro: generate a CL where the opening comes back empty (e.g. app 2810 before manual fill). Verified live via relay audit (2810 opening was "" pre-fix). Session log: SESSION_LOG_2026-07-29_CL_LOAD_2810_GUIDE.md.
